[+15ms] ======================================================================
[+15ms]  Log file: C:\Users\lette\C_Projects\Remote_Mouse_Pointer\build\click_diag_log.txt
[+15ms]  Every left/right/middle click prints:
[+15ms]    - was the click injected by another program?
[+15ms]    - foreground window BEFORE the click
[+15ms]    - what WindowFromPoint() returns for the click point
[+15ms]    - z-order stack of ALL windows at that point (top first)
[+31ms]    - where the ScreenPointer overlays REALLY are (rect vs point)
[+31ms]    - foreground window 300ms AFTER the click
[+31ms]  Then four decisive probes on the teacher window:
[+31ms]    - WM_NCHITTEST: what the window ITSELF reports for that screen point
[+31ms]    - WindowFromPoint at caption-left / caption-right / client / outside
[+31ms]    - screen RGB sampled at each probe point
[+31ms]    - the overlay thread's focus/capture/active AT the click instant
[+47ms]  If WM_NCHITTEST says HTCAPTION but WindowFromPoint returns a DIFFERENT
[+47ms]  window, the overlay is being skipped by per-pixel transparency (LWA_COLORKEY).
[+47ms]  Once per second a HB line reports the overlay's heartbeat:
[+47ms]    ui_responds = SendMessageTimeout to the app (NO => UI THREAD HUNG)
[+47ms]    gui         = INMENUMODE/POPUPMENUMODE/INMOVESIZE of the app thread
[+47ms]    en/vis/rect/st/ex = enabled, visible, rect, style, exstyle
[+47ms]  KEY LINE for resize trouble: does gui show INMENUMODE/INMOVESIZE
[+62ms]    stuck ON, or ui_responds=NO, at the moment resize stops working?
[+62ms]  Press F9 or close this console to quit.
